## EPM:EPM-035

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-019.md#EPM-EPM-035">EPM:EPM-035</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-035</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Uncertain-event conformance bounds</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM4</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S025</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/b1.md#r-b18e665b1b6c05f7b62d04903e69400a1093005d87588bfded7c3d91574cfec8">Complete shared evidence limit · b18e665b1b6c</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Conformance reference, mapping, diagnosis and authorised interpretation/repair</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>A result distinguishes robust mismatch from mismatch that depends on an unresolved event interpretation.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Represent the admitted event uncertainties and compute or approximate lower and upper conformance costs across supported realisations. Report what holds for all, some or none of those realisations without assigning unsupported probabilities.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Material uncertainty is bounded well enough to express meaningful alternatives and conformance remains decision-relevant.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Flag a local ambiguity or withhold the affected judgement when full realisation analysis would not change a decision.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Defensible uncertainty sets, a compatible reference and a declared computational approximation.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/d1.md#r-d1f4a81e343df280e660d5e2b1aa00a31fa4b92e06e30b67a7fc4ea3ea14a1aa">Complete shared assumptions · d1f4a81e343d</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analyst explains bound semantics; the consumer decides whether the interval is informative enough to act on. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Communicate the reference origin, comparison semantics and rival explanations of mismatch to participants and actors entitled to choose a remedy. Specific receiving decision: Analyst explains bound semantics; the consumer decides whether the interval is informative enough to act on.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst explains bound semantics; the consumer decides whether the interval is informative enough to act on.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>A result distinguishes robust mismatch from mismatch that depends on an unresolved event interpretation.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>An analyst either invents a single complete trace or refuses all analysis when activity, event presence or time is uncertain.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Bounds are only as complete as the stated uncertainty set; combinatorial growth and missing possibilities weaken practical usefulness.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/03.md#r-034af36676c697f446fb39140a70548f426f2d6814f83d846442c9a96e540ae5">Complete shared criticism · 034af36676c6</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-014.md#EPM-EPM-S025">EPM:EPM-S025</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-016.md#EPM-EPM-S043">EPM:EPM-S043</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R021</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R024</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R025</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R066</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>Flag a local ambiguity or withhold the affected judgement when full realisation analysis would not change a decision.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. ## EPM:EPM-036

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-019.md#EPM-EPM-036">EPM:EPM-036</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-036</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Plural explanations of deviation</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM4</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S009</p></li><li><p>EPM-S017</p></li><li><p>EPM-S045</p></li><li><p>EPM-S047</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>STRONGLY_RETAINED</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/f6.md#r-f608b3699902b67c4464fc918eb71b9ad50acee87e590aca6bca077390bea612">Complete shared evidence limit · f608b3699902</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Conformance reference, mapping, diagnosis and authorised interpretation/repair</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>The decision record identifies the evidence that distinguishes the adopted explanation from material alternatives.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>For a consequential deviation, keep plausible data, reference, execution and exception explanations separate; gather discriminating evidence and state which alternatives remain. Match the remedy to the supported cause rather than automatically correcting the process.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>A deviation may lead to blame, redesign, software correction, model change or sanction.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>For an inconsequential exploratory discrepancy, record uncertainty without opening a full investigation.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Trace-back, reference provenance and access to relevant participants or source-system evidence.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/95.md#r-958fa91382ffa2f370260cb9bf52c1eac4ee3ffcbc1556f75c33456611e343b8">Complete shared assumptions · 958fa91382ff</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analyst frames competing explanations; data custodians, process participants and authorised decision makers establish causes and appropriate action. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/f5.md#r-f569214754994c25a2f9968c31862b9bae2f7c61696aaf06cab56445fa1ae60e">Complete shared communication interaction · f56921475499</a></p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst frames competing explanations; data custodians, process participants and authorised decision makers establish causes and appropriate action.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>The decision record identifies the evidence that distinguishes the adopted explanation from material alternatives.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Every deviation is assigned to worker non-compliance, ignoring defective data, obsolete models, implementation faults and legitimate exceptions.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Diagnosis can be expensive and participants can disagree. The presence of alternatives does not imply that all explanations are equally plausible.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/2f.md#r-2f248ac4d486038e8d88d90976edcc16df2410b4c39377a7c7be60d22dd44007">Complete shared criticism · 2f248ac4d486</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-012.md#EPM-EPM-S009">EPM:EPM-S009</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S016">EPM:EPM-S016</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S017">EPM:EPM-S017</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-015.md#EPM-EPM-S032">EPM:EPM-S032</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-016.md#EPM-EPM-S045">EPM:EPM-S045</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-016.md#EPM-EPM-S047">EPM:EPM-S047</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R025</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R026</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R027</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>For an inconsequential exploratory discrepancy, record uncertainty without opening a full investigation.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. id</dt><dd><p>EVOLVED_PROCESS_MINING</p></dd><dt>Candidate origin</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM4</p></dd><dt>Source criticism.</p></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Original form</dt><dd><p>Model/log comparison and model-repair research combined with recording-practice and responsible-use criticism. The reconstructed initial problem is: Every deviation is assigned to worker non-compliance, ignoring defective data, obsolete models, implementation faults and legitimate exceptions.</p></dd><dt>Problem it addressed</dt><dd><p>Every deviation is assigned to worker non-compliance, ignoring defective data, obsolete models, implementation faults and legitimate exceptions.</p></dd><dt>Failure mode it tries to prevent</dt><dd><p>Every deviation is assigned to worker non-compliance, ignoring defective data, obsolete models, implementation faults and legitimate exceptions.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>For a consequential deviation, keep plausible data, reference, execution and exception explanations separate; gather discriminating evidence and state which alternatives remain. ## EPM:EPM-038

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-019.md#EPM-EPM-038">EPM:EPM-038</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-038</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Authorised model repair versus process correction</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM4</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S047</p></li><li><p>EPM-S001</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/fc.md#r-fc40d89eec7b0d6920640c2f7747c985d89ffcd58211fbf1e3927f0851380131">Complete shared evidence limit · fc40d89eec7b</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Conformance reference, mapping, diagnosis and authorised interpretation/repair</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>The chosen correction has a supported cause, a stated change boundary and a checked effect on previously meaningful behaviour.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Decide whether evidence supports correcting data, repairing a descriptive model, changing execution or formally revising an authorised rule. For model repair, preserve unaffected intent and re-evaluate changed behaviour; maintain the prior reference when continuity matters.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Confirmed mismatch reveals an inadequate descriptive model or a legitimately approved change in intended behaviour.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Correct one extraction rule or annotate one justified exception when this closes the issue; rediscovery is not obligatory.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Established diagnosis, reference provenance, appropriate change authority and a comparison against the prior model.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/f5.md#r-f5d61293223b66db1de4cbef2e888cdd6169238f13671877dd7c3969f2c292e1">Complete shared assumptions · f5d61293223b</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Model maintainer can repair a descriptive model within mandate; changing an obligation requires its own authorised actor and process. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Communicate the reference origin, comparison semantics and rival explanations of mismatch to participants and actors entitled to choose a remedy. Specific receiving decision: Model maintainer can repair a descriptive model within mandate; changing an obligation requires its own authorised actor and process.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Model maintainer can repair a descriptive model within mandate; changing an obligation requires its own authorised actor and process.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>The chosen correction has a supported cause, a stated change boundary and a checked effect on previously meaningful behaviour.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>A discrepancy triggers indiscriminate model replacement, or the reference is silently amended to make undesirable behaviour conform.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Local minimality can preserve a flawed global structure; fitting all observed traces can normalise wrongdoing. Smaller edits are not always the better repair.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/7a.md#r-7a2b2e87b9902e2f1b681a3e96edf86e02917266cf102dc2ce4f62a08b992ca3">Complete shared criticism · 7a2b2e87b990</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-011.md#EPM-EPM-S001">EPM:EPM-S001</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S017">EPM:EPM-S017</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-015.md#EPM-EPM-S032">EPM:EPM-S032</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-016.md#EPM-EPM-S047">EPM:EPM-S047</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R026</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R028</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R029</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R043</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>Correct one extraction rule or annotate one justified exception when this closes the issue; rediscovery is not obligatory.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. Current native failure/authority/retention limits continue to apply.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Unresolved questions</dt><dd><p>The correct repair may remain unresolved when evidence does not identify whether the model or execution is wrong.</p></dd><dt>Domain lifecycle scope</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/24.md#r-247ed5e0090845cc5bb9037f00625c4c0f5d62973cd0b4d35ecbd44922dcf426">Complete shared domain lifecycle scope · 247ed5e00908</a></p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>Retain local repair as an alternative, constrained by diagnostic cause and the distinct authority of descriptive versus normative revision. Specific receiving decision: Analyst explains the measures; the consumer supplies the decision priorities rather than allowing a metric package to choose them.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst explains the measures;
